## `AFG-6311-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** IG541-Fire-Discharge Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Data Center HV Transformer Arc-Flash & UPS Lithium Room Explosion Incidents

E-stop activation time: ≤ 5 seconds from incident confirmation. Barrier height: ≥ 1.0 m.

- **Standards:** `ANSI/NFPA 70`, `NFPA 855-2023`, `ISO 9001:2015`, `IEC 62305-4:2010`

- **Role:** Immersion-Cooling Specialist

- **Intro:** Immersion-Cooling Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for AI Supercomputing Data Center HVDC Power Distribution Console — Verify Work Permit and LOTO Status: Voltage reading must be 0 VDC ± 0.5 VDC on all phases.
